2026.11.12 晚报

覆盖窗口：2026-11-11T21:30:00+08:00 → 2026-11-12T21:30:00+08:00 · 更新时间：2026.11.12 21:55 北京时间 · 250 条

窗口：2026-11-11 21:30 至 2026-11-12 21:30（北京时间，UTC+8）｜监控名单 134 个账号｜所有时间均为北京时间。

Executive Summary｜核心结论

本期窗口为 2026-11-11 21:30 至 2026-11-12 21:30（北京时间，UTC+8）。其中 11 日 21:30 至 12 日 06:00 与本日早报《2026.11.12 - X Digest - 早报》窗口重叠：Gemini Decisions / Decision 1 的定价与第三方评测、TPU 8i Max 规格讨论、Anthropic 路演毛利率与算力口径、台积电与存储等已在早报出现的内容，本期仍完整收录以保持 24 小时覆盖。增量主线在 12 日 06:00 至 21:30（约对应美东周三白天至盘后、并延伸到周四清晨）：（1）《华尔街日报》等报道称 NVIDIA 董事会正准备罢免黄仁勋，名单内对盘后跌幅有约 8%（亦有 4%、9%）的转述——这是媒体报道与价格反应，不是公司已宣布的管理层变更；（2）台湾流传 NVIDIA 砍单 30% 与 AI 链普跌，但名单内出现明确否认，同时对 Rubin 进度给出相互张力的口径（“已开始出货并贡献鸿海 2026Q4”对“放量出货滑到 2027、Blackwell 加单”）；（3）窗口最后约 15 分钟，Anthropic IPO 的定价、时间表和一份对招股数字的长篇解读在 X 上密集流传，本摘要未核对官方招股文件，其中收入、亏损、客户数和算力支出不得当作已核实事实；（4）广达、纬创、鸿海的三季度业绩与 2027 年需求/缺料表态，以及台积电设备国产化比例、Kioxia 第 10 代 NAND、AMD Venice 已出货等供应链转述；（5）商务部据知情人士称将限制中国 AI 公司通过海外数据中心远程使用先进芯片，以及韩国玻璃基板、HBM 材料与企业级 SSD 订单的媒体报道。

一、NVIDIA 治理冲击｜报道不是罢免公告，但叙事和盘后定价已经在动

@Loris_Dalleau 先后转述《纽约时报》关于又一段涉嫌高管关系的报道，以及《华尔街日报》称董事会正准备罢免黄仁勋，并给出收盘后约 -4% 和约 -8% 两种跌幅口径。@GavinSBaker 引用盘后下跌 8% 的 WSJ 报道，认为很难论证在调查结束前罢免符合股东利益，并主张调查完成后再进行股东投票；Elon Musk 对其投票主张表示完全同意。@evergreencap3 称不为其行为辩护，但就已知信息认为开除不合理，因其“完全不可替代”。@garrytan 认为罢免不明智，交接很难不带来市值大幅崩塌。@BenBajarin 把冲击强度类比为当年得知 Jobs 患癌，并猜测若真到那一步，Ian Buck 可能出任（或暂代）CEO。@mingchikuo 的中英文长帖是本期对“为什么他难替代”最完整的产业表述：其价值不在于比内部任何人更懂 HVDC/CPO 或 CCL，而在于能把工程师认为可能的技术迅速变成全公司必须交付、供应链愿意跟进激进规格升级的产品；他认为就公开信息看黄仁勋很难完全全身而退，较务实的是降低对其个人决策的依赖并布局接班，随后观察 NVIDIA 的新产品节奏和竞争对手反应。@PatrickToulme 则提出另一种假想：若被罢免，他可能再办芯片公司并成为竞争对手。名单外 @TheValueist 把问题从“是否伟大”转到“在上市公司里是否还能被信任”，并涉及股东诉讼、Megaspeed 和国安相关指控——这些是评论作者的主张，不是本摘要核实过的事实。 @PhotonCap 另外汇编了 Megaspeed/Alice Huang 被指涉及约 75 亿美元受限芯片流向中国、资产与采购骤增，以及新加坡立案等条目，并列出黄仁勋减持计划与危机公关的时间线——这些是对调查和媒体报道的整理，含未证实指控，不能写成已判决的出口管制或内幕交易事实。

分析/含义：必须把三层分开——（1）媒体报道与未完成的董事会调查；（2）盘后价格对报道的反应；（3）真正的管理层变更。目前只观察到前两层。如果调查并不导向罢免，8% 一类的盘后折扣可能修复；如果进入交接，投资上更关键的不是道德叙事本身，而是 mingchikuo 提出的那条产业链机制能否在没有同一决策者的情况下维持：激进规格升级、订单能见度、以及竞争对手是否因此放慢或加快投入。Ian Buck“可能暂代”只是个人猜测。把婚外情、走私或间谍指控写成已认定事实，或把“董事会准备罢免”写成“已经罢免”，都不符合现有公开来源。

二、供应信号打架｜30% 砍单传闻 vs 广达“订单未改”、Rubin“已出货”vs“放量推迟到 2027”

@jukan05 称午饭后台湾开始流传 NVIDIA 把订单砍了 30%，CCL/PCB 到代工股普跌；他认为 30% 说不通、像 FUD。随后更新：与基金和供应链沟通后，有人说“CCL 订单砍 30%”为假；他能确认的是 Rubin 再次延期，被告知原因是主板翘曲，HBM 问题据称已在 10 月解决，韩国媒体当天还称 SK 海力士 HBM4 良率达到 70%；因此 Rubin 放量出货滑到 2027，并称 NVIDIA 同时增加了 Blackwell 订单。@dnystedt 则直接回复“我不知道有什么 30% 的订单削减”，同时又写道：Vera Rubin AI 服务器已开始出货，有望贡献鸿海 2026 年四季度至 2027 年增长；鸿海称下一代机柜份额将高于 Blackwell，组装时间从逾两小时降至约 20 分钟，并给出四季度环比和 2027 年高双位数的机柜出货指引。广达方面，CFO 被转述称云客户没有改订单、也没有削减 capex，AI 需求仍强，只是四季度因平台切换会暂时下滑。@SemiAnalysis_ 则从软件/基准侧给出另一条偏积极的 Rubin 信号：称 NVIDIA 与 Inferact 已提交 vLLM Rubin NVL72 成绩，每单位 TCO 性能比 GB300 NVL72 好 6.5 倍，并使用 CUDA 13.4 的 locality-aware MoE。

分析/含义：这是本期最需要避免“选边写成事实”的分歧。30% 砍单目前被名单内两位跟踪者视为不成立或至少未被其信源证实，更像盘中噪声。Rubin 的两条口径可能并不完全互斥——“开始出货/早期机柜、制造节拍改善”可以与“放量推迟到 2027、短期用 Blackwell 加单填缺口”同时成立——但本摘要无法在 X 转述层面把它们压成同一事实。对收入节奏的含义是：若放量推迟且 Blackwell 加单被证实，近中期出货和相关供应链（板卡、CCL、封装、HBM）仍可能更多落在现有平台；若“已开始出货并贡献 2026Q4”被证实为有意义的量，则改代风险小于市场在砍单传闻下的定价。SemiAnalysis 的 6.5 倍是基准提交，说明软件栈在成熟，但不等于出货时间表。HBM4 良率 70% 是韩媒转述，需对照原报道。

三、Anthropic IPO 材料在窗口末尾涌出｜先把转述和已核实事实分开

大约在 21:14 至 21:30 北京时间（窗口即将关闭、对应美东约 8:14–8:30），X 上突然密集出现 Anthropic IPO 讨论。名单外 @Ocean_Yu1 称官方 IPO 网站已上线、招股文件已提交 SEC，高盛与摩根士丹利任承销商。@PenguinWeb3 与一则 @grok 回复给出流通中的发行口径：每股 70–78 美元、发行 8.25 亿股，完全稀释估值约 1.4 万亿美元，定价或在 11 月 18 日、次日以 $ANT 在纳斯达克上市。@gregisenberg（约 76 万粉）发布了对招股材料的 16 条“快速观察”，其中包括：年付 10 万美元以上客户一年增长 12 倍至逾 11,900 家、老客户支出约 9 倍；净亏损 233 亿美元中 219 亿为账面/纸面亏损；上季收入 170 亿美元、同比 12 倍以上；上季算力支出 99 亿美元（超过收入一半），其中 50 亿来自更贵的短期合约；年付 1000 万美元以上客户约 250 家、100 万美元以上逾 1,900 家；账上现金约 960 亿美元、累计融资约 1410 亿美元；人均收入逾 660 万美元；以及 PBC 治理、零售券商申购通道、路演视频和 11 月 18–19 日的时间表。这些全部是该作者对材料的解读，本摘要没有打开或核对 S-1/官方招股书。其中“上季收入 170 亿美元”尤其刺眼，也可能存在口径误读（季度 vs 年化、还是其他定义），在核对原文前不得当成已核实财务事实。监控名单内，@kimmonismus 在窗口末尾注意到招股材料中的一句表述：Anthropic 愿意“比本来可以达到的速度更慢地推进，以便让系统更安全，即便这会使我们处于竞争劣势”，并认为这种写法在 IPO 文件里很少见。这是对文件的摘引和评论，同样需对照原文。

分析/含义：若官方文件最终接近上述收入与客户扩张速度，则市场将不得不重新讨论 Anthropic 相对 OpenAI 的增长位置，以及高增长是否仍被高算力成本和短期溢价采购所抵消（99 亿算力开支 vs 170 亿收入，如果属实，意味着需求跑在长约供给前面，利润率仍受供给约束）。PBC 结构和“不要让公司值超过它真正所值”的创始人信，则是治理和估值叙事，不是近期收入的证据。因为该信息出现在窗口最后十几分钟，监控名单内几乎没有完整评述，主要落在关键词扫描；本节宁可把数字留在“帖文声称”的层面，也不把未核文件写成事实。与早报已出现的约 1.4 万亿美元完全稀释估值、高额筹资目标等路演/媒体口径，应对照同一套正式文件，而不是把多次转述加总。

四、广达/纬创/鸿海三季报与 2027 年约束｜需求仍在，瓶颈是料和内存价格

@dnystedt 转述的三家台湾服务器相关公司是本期最接近“可跟踪财务”的一组，但仍是媒体和管理层口径。广达：三季度营收同比 +125.23% 至 1.12 万亿新台币创纪录，净利 +73% 至 284 亿新台币；服务器已占营收 90%，明年 AI 服务器高双位数增长；四季度因换代会暂时下滑；CFO 称云客户未改订单、未砍 capex；若明年内存仍然很贵，将冲击笔记本和消费电子，公司预计今年笔电出货已因高价跌 15–20%。纬创：三季度净利同比 +148.5% 至 184 亿新台币，营收约 350 亿美元，并核准 134.2 亿新台币 capex 扩台湾与墨西哥。鸿海：三季度净利 775 亿新台币（高于约 696 亿预期），营收 +46% 至约 917 亿美元；刘扬伟称缺料（芯片、内存等）可能紧到 2027，但对明年有信心，能见度比 8 月更清楚；截至三季度末资本开支 1617 亿新台币。另有台积电共同 COO 称台湾设备商已占后端封装设备 20–25%、前端约 10%；Kioxia 计划 2027 年量产 332 层第 10 代 NAND；AMD 2nm Venice SP7 据称已出货。

分析/含义：把这组数字和上一节的砍单传闻放在一起，更像“需求未被这些管理层证实在走弱，约束在组件和内存价格”。广达明确把消费电子而不是 AI 服务器当作内存涨价的主要受伤方，这与企业级需求挤占消费存储的叙事一致。鸿海“缺料紧到 2027”与 jukan05 所转“中国云因 AI 芯片受限削减企业级 SSD、美资云立刻接走供给、大盘不受影响”可以同向理解：瓶颈在加速芯片，存储需求可能在买方之间再分配，而不一定是总量崩溃。Capex 仍在下，但都是转述，需对照正式财报和电话会。

同一窗口后段，@aleabitoreddit 和 @demian_ai 整理了 Nebius（$NBIS）三季报，这是本期 neocloud 侧最完整的一组数字（仍是对业绩/管理层口径的转述）。要点包括：营收单季环比 +68% 至约 9.81 亿美元，AI 云调整后 EBITDA 利润率约 53%，三季度初以来新签/承诺超过 120 亿美元，新单预付款覆盖相关 capex 从二季度的 50–60% 升至约 100%，GPU 小时价格环比约 +20–22%，签约电力走向 5GW。主要负面同样具体：2026 年收入指引落到低端（约 30 亿美元附近），因微软容量交付推迟到 2027 年二季度；并称与第三方 DataOne 的执行问题（临时发电机因许可退役，Bloom Energy 燃料电池尚未完全接上）有关；同时把 2026 年 capex 上修至 270 亿美元。2027 年展望被转述为收入 120–130 亿美元、capex 400–450 亿美元、年末税前盈亏平衡。这对“需求是否还在、只是交付和电力在拖”的判断很重要，但不能把预付款覆盖 capex 理解成已经没有融资或执行风险。

五、政策与材料｜远程使用先进芯片的新规则，以及玻璃基板、HBM 材料的国产化尝试

@jukan05 引用报道称，据两名知情人士，美国商务部正在敲定规则，防止中国 AI 公司通过其他国家数据中心远程使用先进 AI 芯片，并评论 Lutnick 与 NVIDIA 在华盛顿的影响力下降。@tphuang 则把此刻的芯片收紧解释为对华谈判杠杆，并强调钇、镝、铽等重稀土出口下降对日本 AI 与武器供应链的冲击，可能大于“多几颗芯片进中国”。材料侧：三星电机据韩媒已向玻璃基板试点线设备商发 LOI，2027 下半年交付、2028 年目标量产，客户或为 Broadcom；LG Chem 在开发 HBM 用 MR-MUF 材料，试图降低对 Resonac、Namics 等日本供应商的依赖。英国 Nscale 与 CoreWeave 据报考虑在韩国建数据中心，报道将其与美国中期选举后国内数据中心扩张放缓相联系——这是因果推断，不是已证实的选址决定。 @AndrewCurran_ 还转述《华尔街日报》：Elizabeth Warren 与 Chris Coons 要求政府保存与 NVIDIA 对华芯片出口、以及黄仁勋与一名涉嫌中国情报人员关系相关的全部文件。这是文件保全要求，不是调查结论。

分析/含义：远程访问规则若落地，其效果可能接近把“芯片是否离岸”扩展到“算力是否可被中国主体远程调用”，对在东南亚或其他第三地给中国客户提供推理的方案构成约束，也会强化已经在谈的云与主权部署分裂。这仍是“正在敲定”的知情人士口径。玻璃基板和 MR-MUF 材料是 2027–2028 的产能故事，对近季收入影响有限，但若 Broadcom 确实是三星电机客户，则与 CoWoS/先进封装之外的基板路线有关，值得跟踪试点结果，而不是提前计入份额。

Highlights / Top 10｜按投资重要性排序

#1 @Loris_Dalleau · 2026-11-12 06:33 北京时间

要点：转述《华尔街日报》：在曝光与集团一位女性高管的第二段涉嫌关系后，NVIDIA 董事会正准备罢免黄仁勋；并称股票盘后下跌 8%。这是媒体报道加盘后价格，不是公司公告。

为何重要：把《华尔街日报》“董事会正准备罢免黄仁勋”与盘后约 -8% 放在同一条里，是本期治理冲击最直接的媒体+价格锚。必须记住：这是报道和盘后反应，不是罢免公告。

#2 @GavinSBaker · 2026-11-12 07:35 北京时间

要点：认为还应在调查结束后举行股东投票。他写道：在《华尔街日报》报道董事会正准备罢免黄仁勋、股票盘后下跌 8% 的情况下，很难论证这样做符合股东利益。这是对一篇媒体报道及盘后价格的评论，不是公司已宣布罢免。

为何重要：大型二级市场投资人明确反对在调查结束前行动，并提出股东投票；与盘后 8% 跌幅直接相连，代表股东侧的主流谨慎立场。

原帖：https://x.com/GavinSBaker/status/2120661840447635886 ｜ 跳至完整条目

#3 @jukan05 · 2026-11-12 16:39 北京时间

要点：就 NVIDIA 砍单传闻作更新：他能确认的一件事是 Rubin 再次延期——告知他的原因是主板翘曲（motherboard warpage），HBM 问题据称已在 10 月解决；韩国媒体当天还报道 SK 海力士 HBM4 良率达到 70%。因此 Rubin 的放量出货滑到 2027，并称 NVIDIA 同时增加了 Blackwell 订单。与几家基金和供应链接触后，有一种流传是 CCL 订单被砍 30%，他的一名消息源称该说法为假。作者希望这有帮助。以上为作者向供应链核实后的个人结论，仍不是公司公告；其中“再次延期/放量到 2027”需与“Rubin 服务器已开始出货”的另一转述对照。

为何重要：对 30% 砍单传闻做了供应链追问：否认 CCL 砍 30% 的一个信源，同时给出 Rubin 再次延期（主板翘曲）、放量或至 2027、Blackwell 加单、以及 HBM4 良率 70% 的转述。是供应分歧的核心原帖之一。

2026-11-12 05:50 北京时间 ｜ Source: @SouthernValue95 ｜ 原帖链接

很有意思——$GOOG 的 TPU 拥有 AI 计算的最佳架构，而且“TPU 不够用”。

$AVGO $ANT

“今天我会说，大概最好的架构是 Google 的 TPU 架构，因为它从一开始就是按单一系统来构思的。我觉得 NVIDIA 风格的架构正在朝 TPU 架构的方向迁移，但仍然有一些可以替换的部件。然后就是其他所有人——比如，我拿一颗 Cerebras 芯片，再在上面加一个 Arista 交换机，诸如此类。至少在今天，这些系统有效率损失，因为它们不是作为系统被构建的，而更像是拼凑起来的。

我认为行业会演进，但今天，能够把整件事看成单一系统并据此优化架构的人，拥有竞争优势。

看看 Google 怎么做：用这些环形直通的光学 MEMS 交换机——简直就是整个数据中心才是最小单元。但我的感觉是，NVIDIA 也想走到那里。实际上，两个主导供应商都在走向这种系统方法，甚至较小的初创公司也在往这个方向走。

现在 Jensen 是山上之王，极其主导，对吧？Google TPU 非常成功，目前主要由 Google 自己销售——预计这一点会改变。但 TPU 不够用。”

原文：Fascinating - $GOOG TPU has the best architecture for AI compute, and “there aren’t enough TPUs,”

$AVGO $ANT

“Today I would argue that probably the best architecture is the Google TPU architecture, because it is literally thought of as a single system. And I think an NVIDIA-style architecture is migrating in the direction of where the TPU architecture is, but it still has a few pieces that you can kind of swap out. And then you have everybody else — like, you know, I'm going to take a Cerebras chip and throw an Arista switch on top of it, and all that good stuff. At least in today's world, those systems have inefficiencies because they're not constructed as a system — they're more of a patchwork.

I think the industry will evolve, but today, people that can think of the whole thing as a single system and optimize the architecture through that have a competitive advantage.

If you look at how Google does this, with these toroidal pass-through optical MEMS switches — it's literally like the whole data center is the minimum unit. But my sense is that's where NVIDIA wants to go also. 原文：Korean media report that LG Chem has begun developing materials for mass reflow molded underfill (MR-MUF), a key process in high-bandwidth memory (HBM) manufacturing. 关于 Nebius（$NBIS）三季报的整理（均为该账号对业绩/管理层口径的转述，不是本摘要的独立核实）：营收从二季度 5.823 亿美元增至三季度 9.808 亿美元（环比 +68%），调整后 EBITDA 从 2.362 亿增至 4.658 亿（环比 +97%），AI 云调整后 EBITDA 利润率从 49.7% 升至 53.2%，净亏损从 1.904 亿收窄至 3750 万。截至 9 月末 ARR 达到 64 亿美元。管理层口径：三季度初以来新签合同超过 120 亿美元；GPU 均价环比上涨逾 20%，回收期从 22 个月缩短至 19 个月；三季度新单的预付款覆盖相关 capex 的 100%（二季度为 50–60%）；每 MW 合同价值从约 2000 万美元升至逾 2500 万美元；年末签约电力 5GW。称 2026 年有 100 亿美元已承诺客户预付款，2027 年 170–200 亿美元，预付款加上对现有合同的融资大约覆盖 2027 年 capex 的 75–85%。子公司要点：Avride 约 250 辆车、逾 10 万次乘运，目标 2027 上半年全无人 robotaxi；Toloka 收入环比翻倍；Databricks、Datadog、Snowflake 进入企业云；TokenFactory 合同价值环比增 17 倍。主要负面：2026 年指引落到低端（约 30 亿美元附近），因微软容量交付推迟到 2027 年二季度；称与第三方合作方 DataOne 出现执行失误，临时发电机在许可问题下退役，Bloom Energy（$BE）燃料电池产能尚未足够接上。2026 年 capex 从 200–250 亿美元上修至 270 亿美元，同时下调收入预期。2027 年展望：收入 120–130 亿美元，capex 400–450 亿美元，2027 年末税前盈亏平衡。作者认为运营面偏强（预付款覆盖 capex、GPU 溢价上升），近端风险在第三方执行和收入确认延迟。

有不少新闻说 WT（3036）要推出其首款 CW DFB 激光线路卡。

中长期来看，这件事比人们以为的更重要。对经济影响和收入会是什么样：

- $SMTC 74% 的收入来自分销商。再以 $AMBA 为例，其 2026 年收入约 60-70% 经由这家公司实现。

- 具体到 WT，即便看 $AVGO 这样的大公司随规模扩大的情况：

-> 2020 年 Broadcom 收入的 13% 经由 WT
-> 18%（2021）
-> 20%（2022）
-> 约 21%（2023）
-> 约 28%（2024）
-> 现在可能高达约 46%（他们已不再具名披露）

这只是想说明，全球最大的分销商对半导体公司变得有多重要。

- $SMTC generated 74% of its revenue through distributors. $AMBA as another example, ~60-70% of their 2026 revenue went through this company.

- With WT in specific, if you look at even a large company like $AVGO scaled:

-> 13% of Broadcom revenue went through WT in 2020
-> 18% (2021)
-> 20% (2022)
-> ~21% (2023)
-> ~28% (2024)
-> now possibly up to ~46% (they stopped naming)

Just showing how important the largest distributors in the world become to semi companies.

黃仁勳對 Nvidia 的重要性，在於他能把工程團隊認為可能實現的技術，轉化成全公司願意投入資源、承擔風險，而且必須交付的產品。正因如此，供應鏈才能有極高的訂單能見度，並配合激進的規格升級。而這也帶動了競爭對手的跟進，進一步推動整體 AI 產業的技術升級與供應鏈投資。

如果硬要比較，做出並落實激進規格升級的決策，比提供供應鏈訂單能見度更困難。黃仁勳不必是 Nvidia 內部最懂 HVDC、CPO/NPO 與 CCL/PTFE 等新技術的人，但他的價值，在於同時考慮到策略、組織、技術與供應鏈，並迅速拍板定案與投入資源執行。

就目前公開資訊，黃仁勳似乎很難全身而退。在這前提下，比較務實的做法，可能是階段性降低 Nvidia 對黃仁勳個人的決策依賴，同時布局接班計畫。而在這過程中，Nvidia 能否延續高效的新產品與新技術研發，以及競爭對手如何因應，將是觀察整體 AI 產業技術發展與投資趨勢的重點，而供應鏈研究則能提供重要的判斷依據。

2026-11-12 09:47 北京时间 ｜ Source: @mingchikuo ｜ 原帖链接

（作者自己提供的英文版，大意与其中文原帖一致）黄仁勋对 NVIDIA 的重要性，在于能把工程师认为可能实现的技术，变成全公司愿意投入资源、承担风险且必须交付的产品，从而使供应链获得很高的订单能见度并跟上激进的规格升级，也迫使竞争对手跟进。相比给出订单能见度，做出并落实激进规格升级更难。他不必是公司里最懂 HVDC、CPO/NPO 或 CCL/PTFE 的人，价值在于同时权衡战略、组织、技术与供应链并迅速拍板、配置资源。就目前公开信息，他很难完全全身而退；较务实的做法可能是逐步降低公司对其个人决策的依赖并布局接班。此后应观察 NVIDIA 能否继续高效推出新产品与新技术，以及竞争对手如何反应；供应链研究将是重要判断依据。

原文：Jensen Huang matters to Nvidia because he can take technologies his engineers believe are possible and turn them into products the whole company commits resources to, takes risks on, and has to deliver. That's why the supply chain gets such strong order visibility and is willing to keep up with aggressive spec upgrades. It also forces competitors to follow, which drives technology upgrades and supply chain investment across the entire AI industry.

Between the two, deciding on aggressive spec upgrades and making them happen is the harder part, compared with giving the supply chain order visibility. Huang doesn't need to be the person at Nvidia who knows the most about new technologies like HVDC, CPO/NPO, or CCL/PTFE. His value is that he weighs strategy, organization, technology, and supply chain all at once, then makes the call quickly and puts resources behind it.

Based on what's public so far, it looks hard for Huang to come out of this unscathed. If so, the practical move may be to gradually reduce Nvidia's reliance on Huang's personal decision-making while preparing a succession plan. Along the way, two things are worth watching to gauge where AI technology and investment are heading: whether Nvidia can keep developing new products and technologies as efficiently as before, and how competitors respond. Supply chain research will be an important basis for that judgment.

Google 发布了 TPU 8i Max，又称 Whalefish。一个有意思的细节是：此次宣布确认 8i Max 每芯片将有 384GB HBM，意味着它改用 8-hi HBM，而原来的 TPU 8i 用的是 12-hi HBM——这一点我们几个月前就已向 Accelerator Model 订阅者报告过。这追随了 Nvidia 将下一代 Rubin Ultra 改用 8-hi HBM 的做法。

Google 宣传 TPU 8i Max 的推理性能要强得多，因为每个封装现在拥有两倍的 HBM 带宽。带宽对推理性能的重要性高于容量，我们最近在 4-hi HBM 的文章里解释过，可在此阅读：https://t.co/HBWMhtJzy6

原文：Google has unveiled TPU 8i Max, aka Whalefish. One interesting detail is that the announcement confirms the 8i Max will have 384GB of HBM per chip, meaning it moves to 8-hi HBM compared to the 12-hi HBM used for the original TPU 8i, which we reported to Accelerator Model subscribers several months ago. This follows Nvidia's move to move to 8-hi HBM for next generation Rubin Ultra.

Google is advertising much stronger inference performance TPU 8i Max as each package now has double the HBM bandwidth. Bandwidth is more important than capacity for inference performance as we explained recently in our 4-hi HBM article which you can read here: https://t.co/HBWMhtJzy6

市场花了 7 年才终于意识到，苹果的生意是有持续性的，而不是再过一年就会被颠覆、被商品化。而当时亚马逊则被视为云计算的未来垄断者。

2015 年底，苹果的估值是 11 倍盈利。亚马逊 2015 年是 37 倍 EBITDA，现在是 16 倍，略高于苹果估值倍数的一半。市场曾经给再投资的公司更高溢价，并折价对待返还现金的公司；今天则正好相反。

原文：It's interesting to look back at models we had 2010-2020 and what the market believed about $AAPL and $AMZN. The entire market is simply short any belief in any durability of this AI cycle.

It took 7 years for the market to finally realize Apple's business was durable and not just one year away from disruption and being commoditized. While Amazon was believed to be a future monopoly in cloud compute.

At the end of 2015 Apple traded at 11x earnings. Amazon was 37x EBITDA in 2015 and is now 16x, a little over half of Apple’s multiple. The market once paid up for the company that reinvested and discounted the one that returned cash. Today it does the reverse.

Well, the reality is that China actually isn't export less Rare Earth as a whole, but it has significantly reduced export of the big 3: Yttrium, Dyprosium & Terbium. All critical to the most advanced processes.

See below, only Korea got Dyprosium in Aug/Sep & very small qty.

So Trump clearly sees chip crackdown as a point of leverage against China in his negotiations. Jensen was the 1st victim of this but likely won't be last. Lutnick is the obvious next target now that his name is out on Axios.

But make no mistake, this is a true natsec issue now for America. Lack of these 3 heavy RE dragging into next 3 yrs will destroy the Japanese companies involved in AI supply chain as well as the weapon supply chain. That's a far larger issue than a few extra chips getting to China, which Chinese govt is actively trying to prevent!引用原帖（@?）：SCOOP: President Trump authorized a crackdown on companies suspected of diverting Nvidia's advanced AI chips to China on Thursday, but one administration official repeatedly stood in the way: Commerce Secretary Howard Lutnick. https://t.co/5Duzi14eEp 链接

token 可以更便宜，但一项任务仍然很贵吗？

一个 agent 既可能把 token 花在解决问题上，也可能反复花在修补自己的错误上。模型质量、重试、工具和人工复核都很重要。

每美元能买到的 token 数，讲不完整个 ROI 故事。

5/

原文：Can tokens get cheaper while a task stays expensive?

An agent can spend tokens solving a problem, or repeatedly repairing its own mistakes. Model quality, retries, tools and human review all matter.

Tokens per dollar cannot tell the whole ROI story.

原文：- A CREFC report published in November puts vacancy across eight major U.S. data center leasing markets at 1.4%.

- The figure comes from CBRE's June 2026 survey and is not a new November vacancy reading.

- Vacancy fell from 1.6% in H1 2025 but remained unchanged from 1.4% at year-end 2025.

- Capacity across the eight markets rose 33.7% year over year to 10.9 GW.

- Of the 7.48 GW under construction, 80.4% was already preleased.

- In a separate survey, JLL reported 1% vacancy across North America and expects it to remain low through 2028.

- CBRE and JLL cover different markets and facilities, so their figures are not directly comparable.

> Low vacancy despite rapid supply growth signals continued strength in AI infrastructure demand.

> Low vacancy and high preleasing could benefit data center developers that have secured power and land.

> Vacancy measures leasing demand. It does not establish GPU utilization or the profitability of AI investment.

11 月 11 日半导体日报要点（该账号整理）：SMH 收于 506.96 美元（较前一交易日收盘 +4.82%）。NVIDIA 董事会据报准备罢免黄仁勋，盘后跌逾 7%，尚无领导层变更公告，投资问题首先是交接。Fractile 完成 7.20 亿美元 C 轮，有英国主权资金支持，估值超过 50 亿英镑，计划明年制造首款产品级芯片，把 DRAM 直接叠在处理器上。Torus 由 Magnetar 领投获得 5 亿美元，并推出 Foundry，面向 1–20MW 站点，号称合同到算力 12–16 周，用电池和飞轮利用现有并网点的空闲容量。Modine 在得州 Grand Prairie 约 68.2 万平方英尺工厂开始商业生产，针对 2MW 和 3MW Airedale 冷水机组，目标财年底四条 2MW 产线。Meta 基础设施负责人 Santosh Janardhan 将于 2027 年离任，由前 AWS 的 David Brown 接任并兼任 Meta Compute CTO；在 Meta 已给出 1350–1450 亿美元年资本开支目标的背景下，采购与建设将换帅。CoreWeave CEO Michael Intrator 取消了原定的减持计划，称在股价没有实质性变化前，进一步出售仅限于常规税务需求——这不是回购或增持，但减少了可见的卖压。

原文：🔔 The Daily Semi-Cap — November 11, 2026

📊 SMH: $506.96 (+4.82% vs prior trading close)

• Nvidia’s board is reportedly preparing to oust Jensen Huang, sending shares down more than 7% after hours following new allegations involving a subordinate. No leadership change has been announced. The immediate investment question is succession: preserving customer relationships, supply commitments and product execution without the founder who built the ecosystem.

• Fractile raised $720M in Series C funding, with UK sovereign backing and a valuation above £5B. Its first product-scale chip is slated for manufacturing next year, stacking DRAM directly above the processor. The bet: overcome inference’s bandwidth bottleneck without sacrificing the memory capacity needed for large models and long contexts.

• Torus secured $500M led by Magnetar and launched Foundry, prefabricated inference infrastructure targeting 1–20MW sites in 12–16 weeks from contract to compute. Integrated batteries and flywheels help exploit spare capacity at existing grid connections. Bringing GPUs to available power offers a different scaling path from waiting years for new hyperscale campuses.

• Modine started commercial production at its 682,000-square-foot Grand Prairie, Texas facility, dedicated to 2MW and 3MW Airedale chillers. Four 2MW production lines are targeted by fiscal year-end, subject to commissioning and staffing. This moves cooling expansion from announced investment toward actual equipment supply for hyperscalers, colocation operators and dense AI deployments.

• Meta infrastructure chief Santosh Janardhan will depart in 2027, handing leadership to former AWS executive David Brown, who also becomes Meta Compute’s CTO. Janardhan will support the transition through Q1. With Meta already targeting $135–145B of annual capex, the handoff puts procurement, construction and operating discipline under a new technical leader.

• CoreWeave CEO Michael Intrator canceled his planned share-sale program, saying further selling would be limited to normal tax needs until equity pricing materially changes. This is not a buyback or an insider purchase, but it reduces a visible selling overhang as investors scrutinize alignment and financing across the neocloud sector.

为什么 NPO 不仅仅是通往 CPO 的中间步骤
人们很容易把光网络描述成一条直线演进：

可插拔光模块 → NPO → CPO

实际上，这一转型涉及相互竞争的系统优先级。

原文：Why NPO Is More Than an Intermediate Step Toward CPO
It is tempting to describe optical networking as a straightforward progression:

Pluggable Optics → NPO → CPO

In practice, the transition involves competing system priorities.

★@sundarpichai（保留 4 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 5 条）

2026-11-12 02:15 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@sundarpichai ｜ 原帖链接

我们今天发布 Gemini Decisions API，由我们的新模型 Gemini Decision 1 驱动，已在 @GoogleAIStudio 上线。

它不是逐 token 生成文本，而是为封闭集选择而设计，可在单次传递中对最多 1,000 个问题打分，覆盖文本、图像、音频、视频和 PDF。几点细节：

- 3 种决策类型：是/否、多项选择（最多 255 个选项）+ rubric scoring（评分规则打分）
- 输入 $0.10 / 1M tokens，输出 tokens 计费为 $0（比 Flash-Lite 便宜约 5 倍）
- 在多项基准评测上领先，校准误差最低

Google 内部团队已经在用它构建，下面是 Stitch Rapid 在数秒内设计完整产品流程的演示（1 倍速）

原文：Today we’re launching the Gemini Decisions API, powered by our new Gemini Decision 1 model in @GoogleAIStudio.

Instead of generating text token by token, it’s built for closed-set choices scoring up to 1,000 questions across text, images, audio, video + PDFs in a single pass. A few details:

- 3 decision types: yes/no, multiple choice (up to 255 options) + rubric scoring
- $0.10 / 1M input tokens with $0 billed for output tokens (~5x cheaper than Flash-Lite)
- leads on several benchmark evals with lowest calibration error

Google teams are already building with it, below is a look at Stitch Rapid designing a full product flow in seconds (1x speed)（video：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

2026-11-12 00:23 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@sundarpichai ｜ 原帖链接

AlphaGenome 2 正在帮助揭示疾病的细胞层面驱动因素，给科学家一个有力工具，用于预测 DNA 变异如何影响特定细胞类型。

我们身体里的每个细胞共享同一套 DNA 蓝图，但不同细胞类型（例如心肌细胞与免疫细胞）对这套蓝图的解读方式不同。这个新的 @GoogleDeepMind 模型预测这些特化细胞如何解读 DNA，从而把遗传密码转化为生物学洞见。

在 AlphaGenome 1 的基础上，它让研究人员能显著更容易地把遗传发现，与这些发现实际发生的特定细胞中的疾病根源联系起来。

原文：AlphaGenome 2 is helping uncover the cellular drivers of disease, giving scientists a powerful tool to predict how DNA variants impact specific cell types.

Every cell in our body shares the same DNA blueprint, but different cell types (like heart muscle cells vs. immune cells) read that blueprint differently. This new @GoogleDeepMind model predicts how these specialized cells interpret DNA, turning genetic code into biological insights.

Building on AlphaGenome 1 this makes it significantly easier for researchers to link genetic findings to the root causes of disease in the specific cells where they actually unfold.（animated_gif：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

转述 Zach 谈 Ro 五年后“再创立”的文化与经营数据：最好的公司文化是强吸引、强排斥。若干被列出的数字/做法包括：Ro 在美国除夏威夷 Kalawao County（人口 82）外所有县都有患者；2021 年 12 月以约 2.70 亿美元 run rate、同比约 9% 增长；今日 run rate 超过 30 亿美元，连续三年每年增长 100% 以上，员工从 463 人减至 406 人，人均 ARR 提高 10 倍以上；前 100 名雇员中 95 人是创始人本来就认识的人；约 7 名 VP 离职后又裁约 20%；要求全员选择加入或退出新文化，约 6–8% 离开；关掉 10 个以上产品并砍掉逾 5000 万美元不赚钱的收入；七个品牌收成一个 Ro；取消长期路线图、改用 90 天规划；每天 8:50 的会开了两年，成为美国最大 GLP-1 提供商；Ro 是少数收入超过 20 亿美元且今明两年仍增长 50% 以上的公司之一。以上均为该访谈/帖文中的公司叙述，不是本摘要的独立核实。

原文：Zach on refounding Ro after five years of building

“The best company cultures are strong attract, strong repel. It knows who it is, and it continues to try and double down on the features and eliminate the bugs.”

Lessons and surprising stats from how they did it:
- Ro has patients in every US county but one: Kalawao County, Hawaii, population 82

- In December 2021, Ro was growing 9% a year at a $270M run rate

- Today Ro is at a $3B+ run rate after growing 100%+ a year for three years

- It did that with fewer people, going from 463 to 406

- ARR per employee is up more than 10x

- 95 of the first 100 hires were people the founders already knew

- Ro parted with ~7 VPs, then let go of ~20% of the company

- Everyone was asked to opt in or opt out of the new culture, and 6–8% left

- The founders met with top talent for breakfast, lunch and dinner for 30 straight days

- They gave top performers more equity and asked for 90 days. If nothing changed, they’d help them find their next job

- Ro shut down 10+ products and cut $50M+ of unprofitable revenue

- Seven brands were folded into one: Ro

- He told investors that asking for his team’s time would reduce shareholder value

- Zach would sooner consider a 7-day week than a 4-day one

- They killed long-term roadmaps and plan in 90-day horizons

- Every open role was eliminated, and new headcount needs sign-off from the CFO and founders

- A daily 8:50am meeting for two straight years made Ro the largest GLP-1 provider in the US

- Ro is one of a handful of companies with $2B+ in revenue growing 50%+ this year and next引用原帖（@patrick_oshag）：My conversation with Zach Reitano (@ZReitano), co-founder and CEO of Ro

Zach Reitano 谈为什么我们不应把就业置于生命之上：

“每年约有 4 万人死于车祸。这几乎和每年死于乳腺癌的人数完全一样。

无人驾驶汽车已被证明比人类司机安全 10 到 20 倍。所以我们基本上对某些类型的癌症已经有了治愈方案，而讨论的却不是如何为这种治愈做 Operation Warp Speed，而更多是关于就业替代。

我不是想淡化就业替代。而是我们不能把就业放在生命前面。

我们当时在谈治愈癌症，有人说：‘那现在在没有治愈方案的情况下治疗癌症患者的人怎么办？我甚至不知道他们还能不能……’ 这是我听过最蠢的想法。”

原文：Zach Reitano on why we shouldn't prioritize jobs over lives:

"About 40,000 people a year die from car accidents. That is almost the same exact number as the people who die from breast cancer every single year.

Driverless cars have proven to be 10, 20 times safer than a human driver. So we basically for certain types of cancer have a cure, and the conversation is not, how do we have Operation Warp Speed for this cure for cancer. It is more about job displacement.

It's not that I want to diminish job displacement. It's that we cannot prioritize jobs over lives.

We were talking about a cure for cancer and someone said, 'what about the people currently treating cancer patients without the cure? I don't even know that they would be able to.' It was the stupidest idea ever."引用原帖（@patrick_oshag）：My conversation with Zach Reitano (@ZReitano), co-founder and CEO of Ro

如果仔细看 Anthropic 以及整个 AI 行业进展的 AECI 图表，你会注意到所有实验室最近都有一次大幅能力跃升，重置了它们的趋势线。

对 Anthropic 是 Mythos。
对 Gemini 是 Argon。
对 OpenAI 是 Astra。

看趋势线，这些都是一次性的大能力跳跃。现在所有实验室的趋势线又回到了更倾斜的斜线，而不是竖线。从 Fable 5.2 就能看到这一点。它比 Fable 5.1 更好，但并不是 Mythos 相对 Opus 4.8 那种革命性跳跃。

真正的问题是，行业下一次重大能力跃升会在什么时候。

原文：If you look closely at the AECI chart of Anthropic and overall AI industry progress you will notice all labs have recently had a major step up in capabilities that reset their trend line.

For Anthropic it was Mythos.
For Gemini it was Argon.
For OpenAI it was Astra.

If you look at the trend line, these were one-time big capability jumps. Now the trend lines are back to more sloped lines instead of vertical lines for all the labs. You can see this with Fable 5.2. It is better than Fable 5.1 but not the revolutionary jump that Mythos was from Opus 4.8.

The question really is when the industry’s next major capability jump is.

在所有前沿模型发布之外，这件事可惜关注太少。但 Google 今天发布的东西非常重要：

Google DeepMind 的 AlphaGenome 2 可以通过预测 DNA 变化如何影响特定细胞，帮助研究人员为新药找到更好的靶点。

同样的遗传变化，在不同细胞中可能有不同效果。知道它在哪里起作用，可以帮助科学家确定该去研究哪个基因或哪种生物过程。

DeepMind 称，在覆盖 28 种免疫细胞类型的测试中，AlphaGenome 2 识别出了 30% 对基因活动有因果影响的变异，而其第一代模型的适配版本为 16%。

它还复现了一个已知发现：某个 DNA 变异与肠道免疫细胞中的炎症有关。这给研究人员提供了更具体的实验室验证起点。

所以简而言之，AlphaGenome 2 可以通过预测 DNA 变化如何影响特定细胞，帮助找到更好的药物靶点。这非常有用。最近很多人问我，什么时候能看到 AI 真正创造价值：就是这个。

恭喜 @GoogleDeepMind ！

原文：Beside all the frontier releases, this sadly gets too little attention. But its super significant what Google released today:

Google DeepMinds AlphaGenome 2 could help researchers find better targets for new drugs by predicting how DNA changes affect specific cells.

The same genetic change can have different effects depending on the cell. Knowing where it acts could help scientists identify which gene or biological process to investigate.

In a test across 28 immune cell types, DeepMind says AlphaGenome 2 identified 30% of variants that causally affect gene activity, compared with 16% for an adapted version of its first model.

It also reproduced a known finding linking a DNA variant to inflammation in gut immune cells. That gives researchers a more specific starting point for testing potential treatments in the lab.

So tl;dr AlphaGenome 2 could help researchers find better drug targets by predicting how DNA changes affect specific cells. Which is super helpful. And since many people asked me recently when we see real value created with the help of AI: Here you go guys.

Congrats @GoogleDeepMind !引用原帖（@GoogleDeepMind）：Your heart, brain, and immune system share the same DNA sequence. So how can a single-letter change affect disease in some cells, while sparing the rest?

AlphaGenome 2 is our new AI model that zooms in on specific cells to help scientists understand what drives disease. 🧵 https://t.co/CkpKgsutf3 链接（animated_gif：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

2026-11-12 05:47 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

GPT Image 2.5 Sunburst 对 Seedream 5.5，同一场景，连续 15 轮编辑。

我按相同顺序给两个模型同样的 prompt，每一轮都在各自上一轮的输出上继续。红大衣、黄铜灯笼、新的副标题，然后月亮变成地球、燃烧的太阳、冰、铜与金，直到制表匠把那个球体装进袋子带走。

两者都保住了标题完整。差别在周围的一切。到第 10 轮，GPT 版本已经发橙、锐化过度，而 Seedream 5.5 看起来还像我最初的那个房间。

BytePlus 在其 image pack 里的售价约为每张图 0.02 美元。对任何需要大量反复修改的人来说，这才是有意思的部分。

原文：GPT Image 2.5 Sunburst vs. Seedream 5.5, same scene, 15 edits in a row.

I gave both models the same prompts in the same order, and each round worked on its own previous output. Red coat, brass lantern, new subtitle, then the moon became Earth, a burning sun, ice, copper and gold, until the watchmaker bags the sphere and carries it off.

Both kept the headline intact. The difference is in everything around it. By round 10 the GPT version has gone orange and over-sharpened, while Seedream 5.5 still looks like the room I started with.

BytePlus sells it at about $0.02 per image in its image packs. For anyone doing revision-heavy work, that's the interesting part.（video：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

ADM 3 Cloud 改照片比从零生成图片更强。

ADM 3 Cloud 是 Apple 运行在 Private Cloud Compute 上的新图像模型。它随 9 月 14 日的 iOS 27 一起推出，驱动 Photos 里的修图工具（例如 Spatial Reframing），以及 Image Playground 和 Genmoji。

在我们的测试中，它最强的是替换和改动照片中的内容。清理和修复略低于其总排名，重新构图是它的弱点。从零生成图片更弱。

它通过 Apple Intelligence 提供，适用于 iPhone 15 Pro 及更新机型，以及 M1 及更新的 iPad 和 Mac，有每日用量限制。

由于没有 API，我们通过自己基于 Image Playground 做的 Mac 应用来测试。Apple 拒绝了我们 8% 的 prompt。我们只评估了它实际返回的图像。

原文：ADM 3 Cloud edits a photo better than it makes one from scratch

ADM 3 Cloud is Apple's new image model on Private Cloud Compute. It shipped with iOS 27 on September 14 and powers the photo editing tools in Photos, like Spatial Reframing, plus Image Playground and Genmoji.

In our testing, it's strongest at swapping and changing things in a photo. Clean-ups and fixes land a little below its overall rank, and reframing a shot is its weak spot. Making images from scratch is weaker again.

It's available through Apple Intelligence on iPhone 15 Pro and later, and on M1 or later iPads and Macs, with daily usage limits.

With no API, we tested it through a Mac app we built on Image Playground. Apple refused 8% of our prompts. We evaluated it on only the images it returned.所回复原帖（@ArtificialAnlys）：Ever wondered how good the AI that edits your iPhone photos really is? We put Apple's ADM 3 Cloud through our arena: #42 on AA-Image-Editing v2.1 and #85 on AA-Image-T2I v2.1 🧵 链接

2026-11-12 02:18 北京时间 ｜ Source: @ArtificialAnlys ｜ 原帖链接

Google 发布了其首个 decision 模型 Gemini Decision 1，在 Artificial Analysis Decision Index 上得到 66 分，并在知识和推理评测中领先。

来自 @GoogleDeepMind 的 Gemini Decision 1 在 Artificial Analysis Decision Index v2.1 上得到 66 分，与 Elephant 9B（@baselabs）和 Jev 1.14（@typesafeai）相当，落后领先的 Drex Max v1.1（@NaceAI）和 GLiDE v1.2（@fastinoAI）7 分（后两者均为 73 分）。其定价为每 100 万 input tokens 0.10 美元，在该 Index 上的 Cost per 1M Tasks 为 345 美元，由于定价更高，约为 Drex Max v1.1（103 美元）的三倍。

Gemini Decision 1 的主要结果：

➤ 尚未达到 Decision Index 的前沿：Gemini Decision 1 在 Artificial Analysis Decision Index 上为 66 分。它在知识和推理评测上领先，AA-Omniscience-Decision 为 66 分，HLE-MultiChoice 为 49 分。在其他评测上落后，包括我们新的专有分类与抽取基准 AA-Paperclip：它在 33% 的文档上 15 道题全部答对，而 Drex Max v1.1 为 63%。

➤ Cost per Task 是领先模型的 3 倍以上：Gemini Decision 1 的 Cost per 1M Tasks 为 345 美元，相比 Drex Max v1.1 的 103 美元、Jev 1.14 的 156 美元。这一差距来自其 token 价格：每 100 万 input tokens 0.10 美元，而 Drex Max v1.1 为 0.03 美元。Gemini Decision 1 的 Cost per 1M Tasks 与 GPT-6 Luna（Decisions，339 美元）相近。

➤ 推理任务上的校准较好：我们用 Brier score 衡量校准，即模型给所选答案分配的概率与该答案是否正确之间差的平方的平均值（越低越好）。Gemini Decision 1 在整个 Index 上的 Brier score 为 0.18，介于 Drex Max v1.1（0.16）和 Jev 1.14（0.19）之间。校准因任务而异。它在 HLE-MultiChoice 上的 Brier score 为 0.20，属于该 Index 所有模型中最低的之一，而它在 When2Call 上的 0.14，相比 Drex Max v1.1 的 0.04。

原文：Google has released its first decision model, Gemini Decision 1, scoring 66 on the Artificial Analysis Decision Index and leading in knowledge and reasoning benchmarks

Gemini Decision 1 from @GoogleDeepMind scores 66 on the Artificial Analysis Decision Index v2.1, in line with Elephant 9B (@baselabs) and Jev 1.14 (@typesafeai), and 7 points behind leaders Drex Max v1.1 (@NaceAI) and GLiDE v1.2 (@fastinoAI), which both score 73. It is priced at $0.10 per 1M input tokens and costs $345 per 1M Tasks across the Index, around three times higher than Drex Max v1.1 ($103) due to its higher pricing

Key results for Gemini Decision 1:

➤ Not yet frontier on Decision Index: Gemini Decision 1 scores 66 on the Artificial Analysis Decision Index. It leads knowledge and reasoning benchmarks, scoring 66 on AA-Omniscience-Decision and 49 on HLE-MultiChoice. It trails on other evaluations, including our new proprietary classification and extraction benchmark AA-Paperclip, where it answers all 15 questions correctly on 33% of documents vs. 63% for Drex Max v1.1

➤ More than 3x the Cost per Task of leading models: Gemini Decision 1 costs $345 per 1M Tasks, compared with $103 for Drex Max v1.1, $156 for Jev 1.14. This difference is driven by its token price of $0.10 per 1M input tokens vs. $0.03 for Drex Max v1.1. Gemini Decision 1 has a similar Cost per 1M Tasks to GPT-6 Luna (Decisions, $339)

➤ Strong calibration for reasoning tasks: We measure calibration with the Brier score, the average squared gap between the probability a model assigns to its chosen answer and whether that answer was correct (lower is better). Gemini Decision 1 has a Brier score of 0.18 across the Index, between Drex Max v1.1 (0.16) and Jev 1.14 (0.19). Calibration varies by task. with its Brier score of 0.20 on HLE-MultiChoice among the lowest of all models in the Index, while its 0.14 on When2Call compares with 0.04 for Drex Max v1.1

随着 Theranos 和 Elizabeth Holmes 再次进入讨论，我们重温其中一份最出色的、关于公司内部实际发生了什么的第一手叙述。

Tyler Shultz 刚从斯坦福毕业就加入了 Theranos，当时真心相信它的使命。他的祖父、前国务卿 George Shultz 是公司董事，Elizabeth Holmes 成了他的导师，甚至出现在家庭聚会中。但当 Tyler 开始直接接触 Theranos 的技术后，他发现实验室里的现实与公司对外讲述的完全不同。

在 The Social Radars 节目中，Tyler 与 Jessica Livingston 和 Carolynn Levy 坐下来，完整讲述了他如何从员工变成吹哨人：他发现的失败血液检测和危险信号、让员工保持沉默的恐惧文化、他试图在内部以及向监管机构提出担忧，以及在向《华尔街日报》发声后面临的巨大法律压力。

这期节目最初录于 2024 年 12 月 4 日。

00:00 — Tyler Shultz 的 Theranos 故事
01:53 — 遇见 Elizabeth Holmes
04:24 — 最初的危险信号

原文：With Theranos and Elizabeth Holmes back in the conversation, we’re revisiting one of the most remarkable firsthand accounts of what actually happened inside the company.

Tyler Shultz joined Theranos fresh out of Stanford as a true believer in its mission. His grandfather, former Secretary of State George Shultz, sat on the company’s board, and Elizabeth Holmes had become a mentor and even a presence at family gatherings. But once Tyler began working directly with Theranos’s technology, he discovered that the reality inside the lab looked very different from what the company was telling the world.

On The Social Radars, Tyler sat down with Jessica Livingston and Carolynn Levy to tell the full story of how he went from employee to whistleblower: the failed blood tests and red flags he uncovered, the culture of fear that kept employees silent, his attempts to raise concerns internally and with regulators, and the extraordinary legal pressure he faced after speaking to The Wall Street Journal.

This episode was originally recorded on December 4, 2024.

[Anthropic] 2026-11-12 21:20 北京时间 ｜ Source: @gregisenberg ｜ 原帖链接

该账号（约 76 万粉）对 Anthropic IPO 材料做了 16 条“快速观察”，本摘要未核对 S-1 或官方招股书，下列数字全部是其个人解读，不得当作已核实财务事实。要点包括：年付 10 万美元以上客户一年增长 12 倍至逾 11,900 家，老客户支出约增至 9 倍；净亏损 233 亿美元中 219 亿被其称为账面/纸面亏损（与早期投资者股份升值及可转债等会计处理有关）；人均收入逾 660 万美元；“上季收入 170 亿美元、同比 12 倍以上”——这一口径尤其需要对照原文，也可能存在季度/年化或其他定义上的误读；他们最强调的指标是算力效率，称每 1 美元算力带来的收入较 2025 年初提高 4 倍（作者自己也说这可能是公司自定指标）；公司为公益公司（PBC）；可通过 Robinhood、SoFi、Schwab、Fidelity 和 E*TRADE 按发行价申购；路演是 11 章公开视频；代码 $ANT，预计 11 月 18 日定价、次日上市；案例提到沃尔玛购物助手 Sparky，称经其产生的订单大约大 35%；创始人信写着“永远不要让公司的价值比它真正所值多出一美元”；年付 1000 万美元以上客户约 250 家、100 万美元以上逾 1,900 家；上季算力支出 99 亿美元、超过收入一半，其中 50 亿来自更贵的短期合约，作者据此认为需求跑在计划前面；七名联合创始人仍在公司，希望留住的人每年流失约 1%；上季股票薪酬 11 亿美元；账上现金约 960 亿美元、累计融资约 1410 亿美元。作者声明非投资建议。

原文：Some quick observations on Anthropic's IPO:

1/ The number of customers paying $100K or more grew 12x in a year, to over 11,900. And the existing ones are spending about 9x what they did 1 year ago (wild).

2/ The net loss is $23.3B, but $21.9B of that is a paper loss. They owe early investors shares, and those shares got more valuable, so accounting rules count it as a loss (has to do with convertible notes etc being exchanged)

3/ Revenue per employee is over $6.6M!

4/ They did $17B in revenue last quarter, and that's up more than 12x from a year ago.

5/ The stat they feature most is compute efficiency, where they say they get 4x more revenue per dollar of compute than in early 2025. ( I think this is a metric they defined themselves but not 100% sure)

6/ It's a public benefit corporation, so shareholders are buying into a company that's legally allowed to weigh other goals alongside their returns.

7/ You can request shares at the IPO price through Robinhood, SoFi, Schwab, Fidelity and E*TRADE, which I think is somewhat unusual for an IPO this size, but maybe it's a sign of the times?

8/ The whole roadshow is an 11 chapter video that anyone can watch, and it's cool that they shared it.

9/ The ticker is $ANT. It's expected to price on November 18 and start trading the next day.

10/ One of the case studies is about Sparky, Walmart’s shopping assistant, and they say orders through it are about 35% bigger. What’s interesting to me is that shoppers only ever see Walmart, and Anthropic is the plumbing underneath. Sidenote: I co-founded @MeetLCA an agency that builds experiences like this big companies/big startups so this trend is what I'm seeing too (demand for agent experience products)

11/ The founders' letter tells investors to "never let your company be worth a dollar more than it is actually worth." I haven't seen that in an IPO pitch before.

12/ About 250 customers pay them $10M or more a year, and over 1,900 pay $1M or more.

13/ They spent $9.9B on compute last quarter, which is more than half of revenue. $5B of that was bought on short-term deals that cost a lot more, which tells me demand is running ahead of what they planned for.

14/ All 7 co-founders are still at the company, and they lose about 1% of the people they want to keep each year.

15/ They recorded $1.1B in stock based compensation last quarter.

16/ They have about $96B in cash and have raised about $141B in total.

Note: this is not financial advice. Do your own research. I share things I find interesting about startups and building businesses in real-time.

Whether you're into AI or not, this is a historic moment for tech, AI and the USA.标注：约 76 万粉的个人解读，声称来自招股/路演材料；本摘要未打开或核对 S-1/官方招股书。尤其“上季收入 170 亿美元”可能存在口径问题，不得写成已核实事实。同作者另有一条几乎相同的帖子（2120868887705120850），此处只保留较完整的一条。

[NVIDIA治理（名单外）] 2026-11-12 20:46 北京时间 ｜ Source: @TheValueist ｜ 原帖链接

长文评论（约 3.9 万粉），核心论点是：董事会要回答的不是黄仁勋是否伟大，而是在上市公司里是否还能被信任。作者主张标准远低于“圣徒”，并列举其认为他未达到的底线：与被美国情报官员怀疑为中国国安工作的女性保持关系，而公司又是出口管制的卡口；违反自己参与制定的行为准则；股东诉讼指称他在 10 月 30 日仍向投资者表示与 Megaspeed 的关系正常，而数周后董事会就须立案调查。作者引用《经济学人》“无人不可替代”的结论，认为没有指定继任者、直接下属过多，不是辩护而是治理失败；并主张应迅速将其免职、交给机构已信任的临时管理层。对“对手不会等待”的反驳是：一个正被自有董事会调查、被三个众议院委员会要求亲自出席、据《纽约时报》报道安保曾隔离旁观者并就一段视频提出 NDA 的 CEO，才是北京更愿意看到的结果。作者还称集体诉讼指其在 Megaspeed 问题上误导股东，州养老基金等也在考虑查阅账簿。全文是评论作者的主张与对诉讼/报道的转述，其中多项指控和“应当立刻免职”的结论均未被本摘要核实，不得写成已判决事实。文首同时标记了 $MU $SNDK $LITE，但正文几乎只讨论 NVIDIA 治理。

原文：$NVDA $MU $SNDK $LITE This gets the question exactly backwards. Nobody disputes that Jensen Huang is one of the great entrepreneurs of the age. The question before Nvidia's board is not whether he is great. It is whether he can still be trusted, and in a public company trust is not a sentiment. It is the entire basis on which strangers hand over their capital.

No one is demanding sainthood. The standard here is far lower, and he failed it. Do not carry on a relationship with a woman US intelligence officials suspect of working for China's Ministry of State Security while your company is the chokepoint of American export controls. Do not violate conduct policies you helped write. Do not tell investors, as a shareholder suit now alleges he did on October 30, that the relationship with Megaspeed was normal, weeks before your own board has to open an investigation. That is not sainthood. That is the floor for running a public company.

Thirty years of building earns gratitude. It does not earn immunity from the rules he set himself. The presumption belongs to founders who comply. He spent his.

The Economist is right that no one is irreplaceable, and its conclusion, that he should go, follows from the same fact. The strongest evidence is his own org chart: dozens of direct reports and no designated successor is not a defense of the man, it is an indictment of the governance he built. A company that cannot survive its CEO has been mismanaged at the top, and the remedy is not to keep him forever.

The board should do it quickly and cleanly. Remove him now, hand the company to interim leadership the institution already trusts, and let Nvidia start healing. Every week of drift deepens the discount, widens the investigations, and prolongs the spectacle. A swift and seamless transition is how a great but fragile company becomes a durable and effective enterprise, one that no longer depends on a single man.

The argument that our adversaries will not wait gets it backwards too. Consider what they are actually holding. A CEO under investigation by his own board, wanted in person by three House committees, whose security detail, on the Times reporting, sequestered a bystander and offered an NDA over a video at a company event. A compromised and blackmailable chief executive atop America's most strategic company is the outcome Beijing would choose. His departure is not the national security risk. His remaining is.

And shareholders, who are told they would want him to continue? They are suing. The class action alleges he misled them about Megaspeed, and state pension funds are among the shareholders weighing demands for the company's books. The owners of the capital are not asking that he continue. They are asking what he knew, and when.

Greatness does not guarantee goodness, as if goodness were optional equipment. For a man holding other people's $4.5 trillion, goodness, meaning honesty, compliance, and judgment, is the job. He has none of the moral authority left that leading requires, and no board, pension fund, or endowment can pretend otherwise.标注：约 3.9 万粉的评论文章，不是公司、监管或法院文件；文中涉及国安、股东诉讼和安保细节，均属作者转述或主张。该帖主体是 NVIDIA 治理，因出现在窗口末尾的相关讨论中而一并收录，不是 Anthropic 公司消息。

名单外补充（去重后 6 条，均已在上方逐条译出）：@gregisenberg（约 76 万粉）对 Anthropic 招股/路演材料做了 16 条观察，含客户数、亏损构成、据称的上季收入与算力开支等，本摘要未核对 S-1，尤其“上季收入 170 亿美元”不得写成已核实事实；@grok 与 @PenguinWeb3 转述流通中的发行口径（70–78 美元、8.25 亿股、完全稀释约 1.4 万亿美元，或按 70 美元对应发行额至少约 570 亿美元，定价/上市日约 11 月 18–19 日）；@Ocean_Yu1（约 1.2 万粉）称官方 IPO 网站已上线、文件已提交 SEC、高盛与摩根士丹利任承销商，未附文件链接；@web3annie（约 7.2 万粉）汇编盘中涨跌并称 OpenAI 单季收入首次超过 100 亿美元、经营利润率 -109%，未附来源；@TheValueist（约 3.9 万粉）就 NVIDIA 董事会信任问题写了长评，主张迅速免职，文中指控均未经本摘要核实。Bloom Energy / Micron / Sandisk / SK hynix / SoftBank / 铠侠的名单外结果，未见明显高于盘中口号或已在名单内完整覆盖之外的、可独立采用的新硬信息；相关交叉引用见上。同作者 gregisenberg 几乎重复的第二条未另收录。

Coverage & Exceptions｜覆盖范围与缺口

监控账号 134 个全部扫描（含 @nodiligence、@addyosmani、@Omercheema；@Omercheema 仍归在「无关/无bio」，仅作组织位置），134 个均为 complete（分页至 next_token 耗尽，末页为空终止页），无 unavailable / incomplete 账号；窗口内有原创帖/回复的账号 80 个，保留条目的账号 56 个；窗口内原创帖+回复（不含纯转推）共 1151 条，保留 244 条（另有关键词扫描名单外 6 条）；正文图片/媒体预览 107 张。本期未再对零结果账号单独做 is:retweet 分页复核，因此下表中“窗口内无原创/回复”是指 -is:retweet 查询下没有原创或回复，不排除这些账号在窗口内仅有纯转推。

方法：用 X 公共搜索 search_posts_all（from: 批量 OR，-is:retweet，不另加 -is:reply，start/end 限定为本窗口）分 4 个批次（34/34/34/32 个账号）。API 每页上限为 25，按 recency 排序，各批次均未再拆分，分页至空终止页（result_count=0 且无 next_token）。

时间边界：查询以 2026-11-11T13:30:00Z 为含、2026-11-12T13:30:00Z 为不含（北京时间 11 月 11 日 21:30 至 11 月 12 日 21:30），在窗口关闭后抓取，不以早上 06:00 或文档示例 09:30/12 小时为截止。

与本日早报重叠：本窗口 2026-11-11 21:30 至 2026-11-12 06:00（北京时间）与本日早报《2026.11.12 - X Digest - 早报》的窗口重叠，该时段部分帖子两期均收录，以保持本期 24 小时完整性；本期增量主要在 12 日 06:00 至 21:30。早报本身未改写、未重发。

纯转推（RT）不计入、不翻译；带评论的引用帖计入。

过滤规则：寒暄、表情、无信息量短回复、私人闲聊（韩文生活对话、退伍军人节问候、与投资无关的闲聊等）、重复刷屏和明显推广/喊单未收录正文，但计入下表“窗口内”数量。纯提示词展示、模型演示视频在条目中作摘要或靠后排列。接近重复的帖子只保留较完整的一条。重叠时段已在早报保留的条目沿用原译文，并按补抓后的全文做了必要校对。

长文：对 114 条接近或超过约 240 字符或带省略号、但未带全文字段的候选帖用 get_posts_by_ids 分批补抓，114 条全部返回，其中 33 条带 note_tweet；另从本日早报已补全文的记录中回填/校对重叠帖正文（早报侧标记 115 条较长正文可供对照）。未发现仍被截断且无法标注的投资相关长文被当成完整正文使用；仍可能不完整的译文已标“原文可能截断”。

关键词扫描：9 组标准查询中，铠侠 6 个内容页 + 1 个空终止页、已耗尽（135 条）；其余 8 组为抽样（Anthropic/OpenAI/Bloom/SK hynix/Sandisk/SoftBank 各 5 个内容页，HBM 与 Micron 各 4 个内容页），均仍留有 next_token。另加两组更窄的 Anthropic IPO 查询（5+2 页），同样未耗尽。Anthropic / OpenAI / SoftBank 只覆盖窗口末尾约 5–26 分钟，不能代表全天讨论。入选 6 条名单外帖子；低粉丝账号、未注明来源的快讯与未核文件的数字已逐条标注。未对除铠侠外的关键词结果再做穷尽分页；对上述 6 条中的关键 IPO/评论帖用 get_posts_by_ids 补了全文。
